#119540 3 2005-12-01T14:24:35Z Guiseppi
Go to a map, and copy the level data from the box thingy, and in the game, press \ or ~, and then select all and paste the data into the top box, click outside of the box, and press "L" to load it. Then press "p" to play the level, and make sure caps lock is ON. to reset the level, go back to the debug menu with \ or ~, and press R, also, you can reincarnate yourself where the mouse is by pressing ENTER. Hope I was helpful!

#121646 53 2005-12-10T01:06:32Z Guiseppi
Quote: Eli, Dec 9 2005, 09:26 PM
Quote: Guiseppi, Dec 9 2005, 06:31 PM
Eli, that would be consider stealing, because, although the owner doesn't necessarily notice the loss or suffer any immediate negative effects, the culprits are still taking something that is not theirs without the owner's permission, and using it for their own purposes. This is stealing.
No, it isn't. Stealing is only when you deprive another of something in order to have it yourself. When you 'steal' N, you're not stealing, because you're not taking the data from his computer, you're only giving it to yourself.

Stealing is contrasted with vandalism (where you only hurt the victim) and copying (where you only help yourself.)

And anyway, that doesn't contradict anything I said in the first place. My point was that I wouldn't say that on this forum, because people like you would misunderstand, but couldn't possibly (one hopes) misunderstand what I actually said I said.

Well, Eli, while we're at it, let's get into more technicalities, lol. I looked up the word "steal" on dictionary.com (yes, I know I'm a loser so don't say it, lol) and got the following results:
~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~
To take (the property of another) without right or permission.

To get or effect surreptitiously or artfully: steal a kiss; stole the ball from an opponent.

To move, carry, or place surreptitiously.

To use, appropriate, or preempt the use of another's idea, especially to one's own advantage and without consent by the originator.

To draw attention unexpectedly in (an entertainment), especially by being the outstanding performer: The magician's assistant stole the show with her comic antics.

Baseball. To advance safely to (another base) during the delivery of a pitch, without the aid of a base hit, walk, passed ball, or wild pitch.
~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~
I believe the fourth definition is the one that applies to our argument, and I also believe that this is exactly what happened. An idea can't be taken out of someone else's posession, because you can't control someone's mind, but you can take it for yourself and use it for yourself, which is what this website did, am I wrong?

#129116 99 2006-01-02T00:08:37Z Guiseppi
Here are the results of my judging for the entries to the Tileset Madness Contest that 47% has displayed so far, (in alphabetical order by title for added fun.) I list the title and author, followed by the score for the thumbnail view, the score for the regular view, the total score, and finally, I will give some sentences on what I thought of the submission overall. Some submissions will have longer comments than others. This does not have to do with the quality of the works, but rather with the fact that I have more to say about certain entries than others. If anyone is offended by any of my comments, I apologize in advance, for it is unintentional, and the only goal of them is to give an honest opinion of what I thought of the submissions.


Alarm Clock Kirby777
Thumbnail view: 29 / 50
Regular view: 26 / 50
Total score: 55 / 100
My thoughts: It looks cool, and it does resemble an alarm clock, but it lacks realism, and it is disproportionate. It also lacks detail, and doesnt have any great aesthetic value.

Bottleneck Tileset niccus
Thumbnail view: 35 / 50
Regular view: 29 / 50
Total score: 62 / 100
My thoughts: The design, although fun to look at, is maybe a bit too simple, and isnt very exciting. The thumbnail view, however, does show a nice balance of light and dark.

Brickmans avatar sucker1
Thumbnail view: 35 / 50
Regular view: 39 / 50
Total score: 74 / 100
My thoughts: This is a very good piece of N art, which indeed very closely resembles brickmans avatar. The eyes look strange, but on closer examination of the actual avatar, they do indeed look like the avatars. What brought your rating down a bit was in the thumbnail view, the fact that the top and bottom of the circle representing the outside of the head touched the border made the head appear elongated at the top and bottom, taking away from the aesthetic value. Luckily, and strangely, this problem didnt occur in the regular view.

Epitomy of the Four Corners of the Contests ultima888
Thumbnail view: / 50
Regular view: / 50
Total score: / 100
My thoughts: This entry no longer exists in NUMAs database, most likely because you deleted it for some reason. Because of this, I am unable to give it a rating.

Evil...So...Evil Evil_Bob
Thumbnail view: 43 / 50
Regular view: 38 / 50
Total score: 81 / 100
My thoughts: This is an incredible work of N art, which looks very good in both the thumbnail view and the regular view. The stalagtites and stalagmites do wonders for the aesthetic value of the image. The eyes and teeth are beautifully made, and look great in both views. I only rated you down for two things. The first problem was the nose, which I thought was ugly, and took away from the feeling of the rest of the picture. This problem didnt show up much in the thumbnail, but in the regular view, it did noticeably take away from the quality. A jagged or straight triangle, or something along those lines would almost certainly have looked better instead. The only other problem was with the mouth. On the left side, the bottom of the mouth curves up with the top, however on the right side it does not. This took away from both views.

N-Art 11(original) el_umi
Thumbnail view: 43 / 50
Regular view: 47 / 50
Total score: 90 / 100
My thoughts: I decided to be generous by discounting for the fact that N was visible in the design. This is an absolutely amazing design. Its aesthetic nature, and perfect blend of light and dark make it even better. This is also one of the rare times in which I have liked the regular view much better than the thumbnail view. Although the thumbnail view looks quite pretty, and accentuates the nature of the overall design, the regular view truly brings out the detail that makes this work beautiful.

N-Art 16 el_umi
Thumbnail view: 35 / 50
Regular view: 34 / 50
Total score: 79 / 100
My thoughts: I thought that this was a fairly cool design, and in my opinion, the regular view looked just about as cool as the thumbnail, just in a different sort of way. I did like what you did in the middle of the work, however I thought that the widespread nature of the design made it harder to see it all together as opposed to many separate designs, which is how it appeared to me. Each part looked quite nice, but since it was all so far apart, it just didnt meld.

N-Art 19 el_umi
Thumbnail view: 32 / 50
Regular view: 38 / 50
Total score: 70 / 100
My thoughts: I liked the regular view of this design much more than the thumbnail, mainly because the design was quite detailed, thereby making it more difficult to fully observe the full value of the artwork. Up close, however, the design looks quite nice. The outer border does a good job of accentuating the central design. The only real problem with the design is that it looks just a bit too pixely.

Regular view: 48 / 50
Total score: 97 / 100

#6874 1 2006-02-15T14:22:03Z Guiseppi
Okay, I don't know how popular this will be, but I think it's a fun idea. It's another "member above you" thread, and the goal is to make an acronym out of the username of the person above you. In the off chance that you don't know what an acronym is, it is a word in which each of the letters stand for another word. For example, "laser" is an acronym which stands for "light amplification by stimulated emission of radiation." "NASA" stands for "National Aeronautics and Space Administration." You get the idea.
Underscores and other symbols such as periods, ampersands, etc should be skipped ("Monkey_of_Doom" should be treated as "monkeyofdoom"). If there are numbers at the end of the username, they can either be dropped, or the numbers can be turned into their first letter ("Jiggerjaw12358" can either be treated as "jiggerjaw" or "jiggerjawottfe". "7654321" can be treated as "ssfftto", etc...). Prepositions and/or conjuctions may be added in addition to the letters in the username (just as is done in "laser" and "NASA").
Try to make the acronym funny, or make it have something to do with that person's personality, something s/he has done, etc... If you don't know that person well, you can just think up something you feel is funny. As this does require quite a bit of thinking, putting a group of words together that remotely makes sense would suffice, as I know it's not too easy. Some examples would be:

iangb - I am never going back
Guiseppi - Guy undertaking impious spamming escapade proves pitifully irksome. (Wow, I just thought that one up. I'm impressed with myself)

Anyways, you get the idea. I hope you enjoy this.

I was basically just responding to the people who didn't see it as a big deal, and just essentially passed it off as nothing. I was mainly offended by McP3000's comment, but I know I shouldn't be threatened by it, since he is apparently too stupid to spell "Fascism" correctly. Other than that, basically what I was trying to prevent was people looking at it and saying it's no big deal, because it is a huge deal. And in a way, McP3000 was trying to force his opinion on people. He gave some irrelevant, and ironically false, reason that people shouldn't be offended. And then he saved himself: "unless your jewish, then you can be." His apparent lack of intelligence seems to be prevalent amongst the people who see these maps as no big deal, and also amongst the two creators of the maps themselves. Although I don't know about Scott0000; he seemed eerily competent to be posting such garbage. Anyways, I wasn't trying to force people to be offended, although I guess I kind of did say that; I was just trying to prevent people from passing it off like something unimportant.

I don't know if he drank it, but I used it, and damn, that's unsettling. I still don't understand, however, why he was arrested for it. Is there a law preventing the ingestion of others' urine? But honestly, what are the odds? That's incredible. Possibly even the only interesting thing to ever happen in the history of this boring-ass place of Columbus, Ohio.
